### 1. Restore the error-extraction implementation expected by the new triage tests

**Failure:** With the triage dependency installed, `test_build_test_script_preserves_workload_exit_code` calls `generate_jet_trigger_job.build_test_script`, but that helper does not exist. The enabled CLI case also passes `--enable-error-extraction`, which the generator does not accept. These newly added [tests](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/test_utils/test_ci_triage.py#L53-L119) therefore fail with an AttributeError and an unknown-option error, respectively. The [generator](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/test_utils/python_scripts/generate_jet_trigger_job.py#L220-L238) still publishes only `results/`, while the new [report adapter](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/test_utils/python_scripts/linear_ci.py#L169-L178) requests `error_report.json`.

**Correction:** Implement `build_test_script`, add the opt-in Click option and corresponding `main` parameter, and select the wrapped script/artifacts only when enabled. The wrapper must save `${PIPESTATUS[0]}` immediately after the workload/tee pipeline, attempt extraction, and exit with the saved workload status—even when extraction fails. Enable the option in the unit, integration, functional, and smoke generator argument arrays, as required by the [new wiring assertions](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/test_utils/test_ci_triage.py#L150-L157). This requires dependent changes to the currently unchanged [integration stage](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/.gitlab/stages/03.integration-tests.yml#L47-L59), as well as the changed unit and functional stages.

The unambiguous generator wiring is:

```python
# Add to the Click decorators and to main's parameters, respectively:
@click.option("--enable-error-extraction", is_flag=True, default=False)
# enable_error_extraction: bool = False,

# Replace the generated workload job's script/artifacts entries:
"script": [
    build_test_script(" ".join(script))
    if enable_error_extraction
    else " ".join(script)
],
"artifacts": {
    "paths": ["results/"] + (
        ["jet_workload.log", "error_report.json"]
        if enable_error_extraction
        else []
    ),
    "when": "always",
},
```

The extractor's exact executable belongs to the pinned external package, whose implementation is not mounted here; verify that invocation rather than assuming a command name. This snippet is a suggestion, not an executed patch.

**Preservation and acceptance:** Retain the disabled-option case's plain workload command and exact `["results/"]` artifact list. Keep empty-workload placeholder generation and existing launcher arguments unchanged. The existing enabled/disabled tests must both return CLI exit code 0 and assert their respective artifact lists. Add shell checks where the workload exits 7 and extraction exits 0 or 1: both wrappers must exit 7. A successful workload must still exit 0 when extraction fails. Retain the test's assertions for tee logging, immediate `PIPESTATUS` capture, extraction output, and final saved-status exit.

### 2. Make NCCL KV source election identical on both sides

**Failure:** In the new [NCCL backend](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/megatron/core/inference/disaggregation/transfer_backends/nccl.py#L173-L197), a sender plans with only its own source layout, whereas the receiver plans with the complete source roster. The [planner](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/megatron/core/inference/disaggregation/kv_reshard.py#L138-L165) elects the smallest global rank among replicas. Consequently, with identical TP1/PP1 source shards on ranks 0 and 1 and a destination on rank 2, both senders post sends, but rank 2 posts receives only from rank 0. Rank 1's transfer has no matching receive and cannot complete normally. This is a backend API counterexample; I did not establish an integrated production-server path or broad production impact.

**Correction:** Give the push side the complete source roster or an explicitly agreed elected-source set, and filter the same election/reshard plan on both sides. Update `begin_push_blocks` and its metadata contract accordingly. The invariant is one matching receive for every posted send, with exactly one elected source per replicated KV shard. The current push API has no complete source roster, so changing only the singleton planner call cannot implement that invariant; an exact local-only patch would be misleading.

**Preservation:** Do not deduplicate destination replicas: each destination still needs its own complete KV. Retain heterogeneous TP head slicing, explicit PP layer windows, and the existing nonreplicated TP2→TP1 path. Keep this correction confined to KV source election rather than blanket-changing the separate conv/recurrent SSM enumeration.

**Acceptance:** Add a mocked enumeration test with source ranks 0 and 1 sharing one shard and destination rank 2: rank 0 must post the required sends, rank 1 must post none, and receiver operations must match the sends in count, peer, shape, and order. Reverse source-roster order and expect the same elected rank. With two destination replicas, both must receive complete data. Retain the real-GPU [TP2→TP1 test](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/unit_tests/inference/test_nccl_transfer_backend.py#L97-L138), including `torch.equal(buf, expected)`, and add a bounded-time replicated-source transfer check.

### 3. Restore exception-safe MIMO teardown

**Failure:** [MIMO main](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/examples/mimo/pretrain_mimo.py#L81-L148) now performs cleanup only after `pretrain` returns successfully. A setup or training exception bypasses topology destruction and distributed shutdown; after prefetch starts, it also bypasses stopping that worker. The [base implementation](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/f80139c3efc3c0a03c9669df5db5e310f68ad4c7/examples/mimo/pretrain_mimo.py#L71-L107) guaranteed teardown through nested `finally` blocks. A minimal reproduction is a stubbed `pretrain` that raises after the data provider starts its loader: none of the three cleanup calls runs.

**Correction:** Initialize `topology = None` alongside `prefetch_loader`, place the current initialization-through-`pretrain` block inside `try`, and replace the trailing cleanup with:

```python
finally:
    try:
        if prefetch_loader is not None:
            prefetch_loader.close()
    finally:
        try:
            if topology is not None:
                topology.destroy()
        finally:
            shutdown_distributed()
```

Target `main`'s lines 81–148; the setup/data-provider/pretrain statements remain unchanged apart from indentation. This restores cleanup on success, partial setup failure, and training failure, while attempting distributed shutdown even if earlier cleanup raises. Verify this suggested restructuring before adopting it.

**Preservation and acceptance:** Preserve cleanup order—loader, topology, distributed state—and the loader's [bounded, idempotent close](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/examples/mimo/training/encoder_prefetch.py#L392-L412). Add mocked entrypoint tests for successful training, a setup exception before topology assignment, and a training exception with a started loader. Assert exactly the applicable cleanup calls and their order; assert the original training exception propagates when cleanup succeeds. When loader close or topology destruction raises, distributed shutdown must still be attempted. Retain the existing [stuck-worker test](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/unit_tests/models/mimo/test_mimo_encoder_prefetch.py#L356-L383): close warns, does not synchronize the producer stream, and does not raise merely because its join timed out.

### 4. Add the required kernel-level bit-exact extraction tests

**Gap:** The PR introduces [two Triton extraction kernels](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/megatron/core/ssm/ops/intermediate_extraction.py#L38-L175), used by [Mamba prefix-cache extraction](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/megatron/core/ssm/mamba_mixer.py#L1049-L1069). Their new [correctness tests](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/unit_tests/ssm/ops/test_ssd_combined.py#L349-L464) make single reference comparisons; they do not compare repeated executions bit-exactly. The [determinism suite](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/tree/87e9c7f5d0e6412ab29842dddacf0e536dd83de4/tests/unit_tests/determinism) has neither the kernel-test subdirectory nor its manifest required by the pinned light rubric. This is a coverage finding, not evidence that either kernel is nondeterministic.

**Correction:** Add kernel-level repeated-execution tests and register both extraction kernels in the required kernel manifest. Keep the kernel implementation unchanged unless those tests demonstrate a failure. The referenced determinism guide and registry schema are absent from the mounted base/head, so I cannot safely prescribe their exact integration code.

**Preservation and acceptance:** For each kernel, run identical immutable inputs into independently sentinel-initialized outputs and compare output bytes exactly after synchronization. Cover runtime counts 0, partial, and full; repeated/nonmonotonic SSM indices; and the supported state dtypes. Capture once and replay with changed runtime counts, checking that padded slots remain untouched. Retain the existing conv-clamp case `pos=2, d_conv=4`, whose window must read token positions `[0, 0, 0, 1]`, and the existing real-count sentinel assertions. These are proposed regression checks; they were not executed.

### F2 — Make NCCL KV source election agree between sender and receiver

**Failure:** Give source ranks 0 and 1 identical TP1/PP1 KV shards, and destination rank 2 one complete shard. Call each sender's `begin_push_blocks` with rank 2's metadata, and call the receiver's `begin_pull_blocks` with both source metadata records. The [sender enumeration](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/inference/disaggregation/transfer_backends/nccl.py#L173-L197) plans with only its own source layout, so both senders post sends. The receiver supplies both sources to the [planner's smallest-rank election](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/inference/disaggregation/kv_reshard.py#L138-L165), so it receives only from rank 0. Rank 1 consequently has an unmatched send, and its handle cannot complete normally. Expected: exactly one elected source per replicated shard, with every posted send matched by a receive. This is a backend API counterexample; an integrated production-server path was not established.

**Correction:** Change `_kv_transfers` and the [push metadata contract](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/inference/disaggregation/transfer_backends/nccl.py#L267-L293) so the push side receives the complete source roster or an explicitly agreed elected-source set. Both sides must use the same source election and filter transfers for their own rank. Update metadata producers and direct callers to supply that information. The current push contract carries destination metadata only, so replacing the singleton planner argument alone is insufficient; an exact local-only patch would be misleading.

**Preservation:** Deduplicate source replicas, **not destination replicas**: every destination still needs complete KV. Retain nonreplicated TP2→TP1 head merging, explicit PP layer windows, block ordering, and TP/PP metadata normalization. Do not blanket-change the separate conv/recurrent SSM enumeration.

**Acceptance — proposed checks:** Using the existing test geometry and block IDs `[1, 3]`, mock P2P posting for the replicated-source setup above. Rank 0 must post the required sends, rank 1 none, and receiver operations must match send peers, shapes, counts, and per-peer order. Reversing the source roster must elect rank 0 again. With two destination replicas, both must receive complete data. Retain the [real-GPU TP2→TP1 test](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/tests/unit_tests/inference/test_nccl_transfer_backend.py#L97-L138), specifically `torch.equal(buf, expected)` and the final all-rank success assertion; add a bounded-time replicated-source transfer check.

### F3 — Restore exception-safe MIMO teardown

**Failure:** In [MIMO `main`](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/examples/mimo/pretrain_mimo.py#L81-L148), cleanup follows `pretrain` only on normal return. A setup exception skips distributed shutdown; a training exception after the data provider starts prefetch also skips loader closure and topology destruction. A stubbed `pretrain` that starts the mocked data provider and raises reproduces the missing cleanup calls. The [merge-base implementation](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/f80139c3efc3c0a03c9669df5db5e310f68ad4c7/examples/mimo/pretrain_mimo.py#L71-L107) guaranteed teardown with nested `finally` blocks.

**Correction:** In `main`, initialize `topology = None` alongside `prefetch_loader = None`, put the existing initialization-through-`pretrain` statements inside `try`, and replace the trailing cleanup with this suggested block:

```python
finally:
    try:
        if prefetch_loader is not None:
            prefetch_loader.close()
    finally:
        try:
            if topology is not None:
                topology.destroy()
        finally:
            shutdown_distributed()
```

The target is lines 81–148; retain the setup, model-capture hook, nested data provider, and `pretrain` arguments unchanged apart from indentation. The invariant is that every applicable cleanup is attempted on success or failure, and distributed shutdown is still attempted when an earlier cleanup raises.

**Preservation:** Keep cleanup order—loader, topology, distributed state—and preserve the loader's [bounded, idempotent close](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/examples/mimo/training/encoder_prefetch.py#L392-L412). Non-prefetch runs must not attempt loader closure, and failure before topology assignment must not attempt topology destruction.

**Acceptance — proposed checks:** Mock setup resources and record cleanup calls for successful training, setup failure before topology assignment, and training failure after a mocked loader starts. Assert the applicable calls occur exactly once in the specified order; the original training exception must propagate when cleanup succeeds. Inject loader-close and topology-destroy exceptions separately and assert shutdown still runs. Retain [the stuck-worker regression](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/tests/unit_tests/models/mimo/test_mimo_encoder_prefetch.py#L356-L383): close warns without raising on join timeout, and producer-stream synchronization remains uncalled.

### F4 — Add the required bit-exact extraction-kernel tests

**Coverage gap:** The [two Triton extraction kernels](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/ssm/ops/intermediate_extraction.py#L38-L175) are used by [Mamba prefix-cache extraction](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/ssm/mamba_mixer.py#L1049-L1069). Their [direct tests](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/tests/unit_tests/ssm/ops/test_ssd_combined.py#L349-L464) perform single reference comparisons, not repeated-execution bit-exact checks. The current [determinism suite](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/tree/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/tests/unit_tests/determinism) still lacks the kernel-test directory and manifest required by the pinned light rubric. This is a testing gap, **not evidence that either kernel is nondeterministic**.

**Correction:** Add repeated-execution kernel tests under the required determinism-kernel test location and register both extraction kernels in its manifest. Keep kernel implementations unchanged unless those tests expose a failure. The integration guide and registry schema are unavailable, so exact registration code cannot safely be prescribed.

**Preservation and acceptance — proposed checks:** For each public extraction function, use identical immutable inputs and independently sentinel-initialized outputs, synchronize, and compare output bytes exactly across repeated executions. Cover runtime counts zero, partial, and full; repeated/nonmonotonic SSM indices; and supported state dtypes, including BF16 and FP32. Capture once and replay with changed runtime counts: only active slots may change, while padded slots must retain their sentinel. Preserve the existing conv-clamp assertions for `pos=2, d_conv=4`, which must read token positions `[0, 0, 0, 1]`, and both existing real-count gating assertions. Also retain the [multi-chunk prefix-cache regression](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/tests/unit_tests/inference/engines/test_hybrid_prefix_caching_e2e.py#L659-L729): the token-768 snapshot must be registered, and the reuse request must match three Mamba blocks.

*Review generated by codex*

<!-- nemo-ci-formal-review:bae482423367815952340f62b6a5a3ca41d14279a35ab37a87c7e766892b1337:dd3a56c092a9d26193cdf556bedef2e42e94f58535cab0ee270afcd037f04f78 -->

- FDecaYed · COMMENTED · 2026-10-09T13:33:19Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/7906#pullrequestreview-5470751474)

Two potential integration issues I found in the consolidated merge at [`d92c81ed`](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/commit/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e). This review is limited to merge-introduced issues, not defects already present on either baseline.

### 1. NCCL-EP zero-copy protection is bypassed by the active overlap schedule

The [schedule plan](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/models/common/model_chunk_schedule_plan.py#L297-L300) instantiates the GPT-local `TransformerLayerNode`, whose [super call](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/models/gpt/fine_grained_callables.py#L362-L370) does not forward `moe_ncclep_zero_copy`. The new protection therefore remains disabled on the active path.

There is a second gap: [scheduled release](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/pipeline_parallel/utils.py#L286-L305) runs before the symmetric-storage check, and its [release action](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/pipeline_parallel/combined_1f1b_tensor_release.py#L358-L365) can attempt to resize persistent symmetric storage to zero. Main's zero-copy buffers and dev's release/ownership machinery need to be integrated consistently.

**Suggested correction:** Forward the flag through the active GPT node and treat persistent symmetric buffers as externally owned, excluding them from destructive scheduled release and incompatible alias ownership checks. If that combination cannot be supported safely in this sync, reject zero-copy + scheduled release explicitly. Verify overlap with scheduled release both enabled and disabled, including buffer reuse.

### 2. GTP-sharded QKV parameters silently lose Muon splitting

The retained [QKV tagger](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d92c81ed6c6230c73409cca5c5415b802811824e/megatron/core/optimizer/__init__.py#L824-L842) reconstructs logical rows using TP only. For example, 8 heads, 4 query groups and head dimension 128 give 2048 logical QKV rows; TP1/GTP2 stores 1024 rows locally, so the tagger sets `is_qkv=False` and silently bypasses the requested QKV split.

**Suggested correction:** Keep GTP and manually adapt the core fix from #7117, which is outside this 100-commit slice:
- Derive eligibility and split metadata from the unsharded, unpadded logical layout.
- Gather before splitting/NS, then restore padding and the local GTP shard.
- Preserve dev #7085's per-head/MLA handling and existing mode defaults; carry required metadata through optimizer master copies.

Please avoid replacing the whole Muon implementation or reverting GTP wholesale: #6057 in this slice depends on #4967. A focused compatibility fix should be enough. Acceptance should exercise the actual optimizer factory and compare matched TP/GTP QKV updates, including padding, per-head and fragmented layouts, with consistent collectives across ranks.

These findings are based on source inspection and focused CPU-only logic checks; no GPU/NCCL end-to-end reproduction was performed. The proposed corrections and acceptance checks have not been implemented or run.

- svcnemo-autobot · comment · 2026-10-07T22:52:56Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/7906#issuecomment-6048489354)

**`/review` — review this pull request**

Run a formal review of the pull request containing your comment.

**Usage**

```text
/review [mode=<light|strict>] [model=<codex|claude>] [reset=<true|false>] [role=<engineer|automation>]
/review help
```

**Options**

| Option | Default | Behavior |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `mode` | Review profile’s default | `light`: high-confidence blocking defects. `strict`: adds edge cases, compatibility, and hardening. |
| `model` | `codex` | Reviewer backend: `codex` or `claude`. |
| `reset` | `false` | `false`: follow up on earlier findings and higher-impact introduced regressions. `true`: request a fresh broad review. The selected skill may override reset interpretation, scope, coverage, and funnel filtering. Boolean values are case-insensitive. |
| `role` | `engineer` | Review focus for this dispatch. The repository selects the main review skill; ROLE=engineer or ROLE=automation specializes that skill alongside MODE without selecting or loading a second rubric. |

**Examples**

Default review:

```text
/review
```

Fresh, strict review using Claude:

```text
/review mode=strict model=claude reset=true
```

**Notes**

- Put the command on one line; option order is unrestricted.
- `/review help` displays help without starting a run.
- The repository selects the main review skill; role and mode specialize its invocation.
- Fallback mode: `light`; `/review mode=` overrides the selected profile's default for this dispatch.
- `role=automation`: Workflow safety, identity and authority, idempotency, retries, and operational behavior.
- `role=engineer`: Implementation correctness, maintainability, tests, and developer-facing behavior.

| F3: MIMO teardown | `a69bbb09` (#5833) replaced the nested `finally` that the merge-base `f41ec549` and dev shared (blob `4f304958`). | `pretrain_mimo.py` is byte-identical to the slice tip. | Cleanup still runs in sequence with no `finally`. 6 later main commits touch this file. | Regression owned by main; fix it on main, then a later sync brings it to dev |

Its `manifest.py` registers `ssm/ops/common/intermediate_extraction.py`. | Already fixed on main; arrives in a later sync slice |

**Why not patch these here:** this PR imports exactly 100 main commits, adds one merge commit, and allows at most one repair commit, limited to merge and compatibility fixes.
- A dev-only fix for F2 or F3 would make dev diverge from main in files main is still changing, so later syncs would hit conflicts.
- An F4 fix would mean importing commits beyond this slice.

The right fix is a PR on main for F2 and one for F3. If maintainers want, I can prepare them as separate work. If you agree with this disposition, please dismiss this bot review. No code changed for F2–F4, so no tests were run for them.
